{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_423fb0d5cc81484c80acc008","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Verdict Definitions\n2\t\n3\tEvaluator verdicts are stable values. Use exactly one.\n4\t\n5\t## PASS\n6\t\n7\tUse `PASS` when all are true:\n8\t\n9\t- approved scope is complete,\n10\t- required static gates pass or are not applicable,\n11\t- required fitness gates pass, are not applicable, or have valid debt entries,\n12\t- required runtime and consumer gates have evidence,\n13\t- no unrecorded doctrine violation was introduced or deepened,\n14\t- docs and run artifacts are updated enough for resume.\n15\t\n16\t## FAIL_PLAN\n17\t\n18\t`FAIL_PLAN` is emitted only by PLAN-EVAL at the Plan-Gate. The plan is incomplete or unsound (an\n19\tunchecked `gates/plan-gate.md` box). Returns the run to Plan & Design; no implementation slice may\n20\tbe committed. Two `FAIL_PLAN` cycles, then escalate to the user.\n21\t\n22\t## FAIL_FIX\n23\t\n24\tUse `FAIL_FIX` when the plan remains valid but the implementation or docs need more work. Examples:\n25\t\n26\t- a required gate fails,\n27\t- evidence is missing,\n28\t- a path/link is wrong,\n29\t- a consumer import was not updated,\n30\t- a false-done state from the profile is present.\n31\t\n32\t## FAIL_RESCOPE\n33\t\n34\tUse `FAIL_RESCOPE` when the approved plan is materially wrong or too small. Examples:\n35\t\n36\t- the task requires a package/API redesign not named in the plan,\n37\t- the selected archetype is wrong,\n38\t- a docs run uncovers code reality that needs a separate implementation plan,\n39\t- external/runtime validation cannot be performed without adding new scope.\n40\t\n41\t## FAIL_DEBT\n42\t\n43\tUse `FAIL_DEBT` when code/docs may otherwise satisfy the plan, but architecture debt handling is\n44\tinvalid. Examples:\n45\t\n46\t- a doctrine violation is introduced without an `arch-debt.md` entry,\n47\t- an entry lacks owner, target, reason, linked plan, or status,\n48\t- new work deepens an existing debt entry without updating it,\n49\t- a run closes a debt entry without evidence that the relevant gate now passes.\n50\t\n51\t`FAIL_DEBT` means the required fix is debt bookkeeping or debt rationale, not necessarily\n52\timplementation.\n53\t\n54\t## Choosing Between Verdicts\n55\t\n56\t| Condition                                                    | Verdict        |\n57\t| ------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------- |\n58\t| Plan incomplete or unsound at the Plan-Gate (PLAN-EVAL only) | `FAIL_PLAN`    |\n59\t| Code/docs incomplete, plan valid                             | `FAIL_FIX`     |\n60\t| Plan/archetype/scope invalid                                 | `FAIL_RESCOPE` |\n61\t| Only debt registry is blocking                               | `FAIL_DEBT`    |\n62\t| All applicable gates and debt rules satisfied                | `PASS`         |\n63\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"815534c7-6c02-4aa5-ab86-a905a0bade6f","uuid":"2cb45819-39a0-4a79-9848-8a761bea5689","timestamp":"2026-08-06T13:02:31.626Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/codex/repos/ns1331-qwen-evaluator/.llm/harness/evaluator/verdict-definitions.md","content":"# Verdict Definitions\n\nEvaluator verdicts are stable values. Use exactly one.\n\n## PASS\n\nUse `PASS` when all are true:\n\n- approved scope is complete,\n- required static gates pass or are not applicable,\n- required fitness gates pass, are not applicable, or have valid debt entries,\n- required runtime and consumer gates have evidence,\n- no unrecorded doctrine violation was introduced or deepened,\n- docs and run artifacts are updated enough for resume.\n\n## FAIL_PLAN\n\n`FAIL_PLAN` is emitted only by PLAN-EVAL at the Plan-Gate. The plan is incomplete or unsound (an\nunchecked `gates/plan-gate.md` box). Returns the run to Plan & Design; no implementation slice may\nbe committed. Two `FAIL_PLAN` cycles, then escalate to the user.\n\n## FAIL_FIX\n\nUse `FAIL_FIX` when the plan remains valid but the implementation or docs need more work. Examples:\n\n- a required gate fails,\n- evidence is missing,\n- a path/link is wrong,\n- a consumer import was not updated,\n- a false-done state from the profile is present.\n\n## FAIL_RESCOPE\n\nUse `FAIL_RESCOPE` when the approved plan is materially wrong or too small. Examples:\n\n- the task requires a package/API redesign not named in the plan,\n- the selected archetype is wrong,\n- a docs run uncovers code reality that needs a separate implementation plan,\n- external/runtime validation cannot be performed without adding new scope.\n\n## FAIL_DEBT\n\nUse `FAIL_DEBT` when code/docs may otherwise satisfy the plan, but architecture debt handling is\ninvalid. Examples:\n\n- a doctrine violation is introduced without an `arch-debt.md` entry,\n- an entry lacks owner, target, reason, linked plan, or status,\n- new work deepens an existing debt entry without updating it,\n- a run closes a debt entry without evidence that the relevant gate now passes.\n\n`FAIL_DEBT` means the required fix is debt bookkeeping or debt rationale, not necessarily\nimplementation.\n\n## Choosing Between Verdicts\n\n| Condition                                                    | Verdict        |\n| ------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------- |\n| Plan incomplete or unsound at the Plan-Gate (PLAN-EVAL only) | `FAIL_PLAN`    |\n| Code/docs incomplete, plan valid                             | `FAIL_FIX`     |\n| Plan/archetype/scope invalid                                 | `FAIL_RESCOPE` |\n| Only debt registry is blocking                               | `FAIL_DEBT`    |\n| All applicable gates and debt rules satisfied                | `PASS`         |\n","numLines":63,"startLine":1,"totalLines":63}}}

R-BASE-L2)\n14\t  - `docs/architecture/doctrine/05-folder-structure.md` (incl. R-FOLD-CARD, R-FOLD-LAYERING-MODE, R-FOLD-AD-COLOC)\n15\t  - `docs/architecture/doctrine/06-archetypes.md#archetype-6--cli--tooling-package`\n16\t  - `docs/architecture/doctrine/07-composition-and-extension.md` (incl. R-COMP-DECL, R-COMP-EXT-MANIFEST)\n17\t  - `docs/architecture/doctrine/09-anti-patterns-and-fitness-functions.md`\n18\t- Anti-patterns: AP-1, AP-2, AP-3, AP-4, AP-5, AP-6, AP-7, AP-8, AP-9, AP-11, AP-13, AP-14, AP-15,\n19\t  AP-16, AP-18, AP-19, AP-20, AP-21, AP-22, AP-23, AP-24, AP-25.\n20\t- Universal fitness functions: F-1, F-2, F-3, F-4, F-5, F-6, F-7, F-8, F-9, F-10, F-11, F-12, F-15,\n21\t  F-16, F-17, F-18. F-14 excludes allowed CLI presentation output.\n22\t- Archetype-specific fitness functions: F-CLI-1 … F-CLI-31 (see §\"Fitness Gates\").\n23\t\n24\t## When This Archetype Applies\n25\t\n26\tUse this profile for packages that ship a binary, command flows, scaffolding, deployment tooling, or\n27\tother user-run automation. The CLI may also expose a library surface (`mod.ts`), but only when\n28\tconsumers legitimately call the flow programmatically; otherwise the only public artifact is the\n29\tbinary.\n30\t\n31\t## Layering modes (R-FOLD-LAYERING-MODE)\n32\t\n33\tArchetype 6 mixes both layering modes:\n34\t\n35\t- **Kernel** is _horizontal_: pure types, ports, application abstracts\n36\t  - registries, adapters, presentation abstracts, output renderers, assets. Each folder names a\n37\t    technical role.\n38\t- **Surfaces** (`public/`, `maintainer/`) are _vertical_: each feature is a folder that contains the\n39\t  command, the use case, the input shape, optionally a pipeline + steps, optionally per-feature\n40\t  adapters.\n41\t- The kernel-surface boundary is enforced by F-CLI-3 (no surface ↔ surface imports) and F-CLI-4\n42\t  (kernel never imports from surfaces).\n43\t\n44\t## Greenfield Folder Shape\n45\t\n46\t```\n47\tpackages/<cli-pkg>/\n48\t├── deno.json\n49\t├── mod.ts                                  # JSR public surface\n50\t├── maintainer.ts                           # gated; never published\n51\t├── testing.ts                              # in-memory adapters, fixtures\n52\t├── bin/\n53\t│   ├── <cli>.ts                            # public binary\n54\t│   └── <cli>-dev.ts                        # maintainer binary\n55\t└── src/\n56\t    ├── kernel/                             # horizontal core\n57\t    │   ├── domain/\n58\t    │   │   ├── errors/                     # CliError + concretes\n59\t    │   │   ├── values/                     # value types, ids, paths\n60\t    │   │   └── <axis-plural>/              # one folder per polymorphic family\n61\t    │   ├── ports/                          # *-port.ts only\n62\t    │   ├── application/\n63\t    │   │   ├── abstracts/                  # spine + layer-2 abstracts\n64\t    │   │   └── registries/                 # one Registry concrete per axis\n65\t    │   ├── adapters/\n66\t    │   │   ├── file-system/\n67\t    │   │   ├── logger/\n68\t    │   │   ├── process/\n69\t    │   │   ├── workspace-resolvers/\n70\t    │   │   ├── scaffolders/\n71\t    │   │   ├── templates/\n72\t    │   │   └── <other-port>/               # one folder per port\n73\t    │   ├── presentation/\n74\t    │   │   ├── abstracts/                  # CliCommand, CliCommandGroup,\n75\t    │   │   │                               # CliRoot, ScaffoldCommand,\n76\t    │   │   │                               # ListCommand, DeployStepCommand\n77\t    │   │   └── output/                     # OutputEvent + renderers\n78\t    │   ├── assets/                         # all .template files; typed registry\n79\t    │   │   ├── manifest.ts\n80\t    │   │   ├── template-registry.ts\n81\t    │   │   └── <group>/                    # contracts, plugins, services, …\n82\t    │   └── extension-points.ts             # R-COMP-EXT-MANIFEST\n83\t    ├── public/                             # vertical features\n84\t    │   ├── composition.ts                  # declarative; R-COMP-DECL\n85\t    │   ├── deps.ts                         # imports of jsr:@netscript/* only\n86\t    │   ├── adapters/                       # public-only adapters (e.g. JSR)\n87\t    │   └── features/\n88\t    │       └── <feature>/\n89\t    │           ├── <feature>-group.ts      # if multi-sub-feature\n90\t    │           ├── <sub-feature>/\n91\t    │           │   ├── <verb>-<noun>-command.ts\n92\t    │           │   ├── <verb>-<noun>.ts            # use case\n93\t    │           │   ├── <verb>-<noun>-input.ts\n94\t    │           │   └── steps/                       # if pipeline\n95\t    │           │       └── <verb>-step.ts\n96\t    │           └── …\n97\t    └── maintainer/                          # same shape; monorepo deps\n98\t        ├── composition.ts\n99\t        ├── deps.ts\n100\t        ├── adapters/                        # maintainer-only adapters\n101\t        └── features/\n102\t            └── <feature>/…\n103\t```\n104\t\n105\tThe shape is normative: deviations require an explicit override in the package's README §\"Archetype\n106\t6 v2 deviations\" with rationale, and the deviation is recorded in `arch-debt.md` until resolved.\n107\t\n108\t## Spine and Layer-2 Abstracts\n109\t\n110\tEvery Archetype 6 package declares the **five spine abstracts** (stub-only, per §3 doctrine):\n111\t\n112\t| Spine class                 | Role                                                             |\n113\t| --------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |\n114\t| `CliCommand<Input, Result>` | A single user-facing command. Owns one verb.                     |\n115\t| `CliCommandGroup`           | A non-leaf node grouping commands by domain (`contracts`, `db`). |\n116\t| `CliRoot`                   | The composition entry. Owns the top-level command list.          |\n117\t| `UseCase<Input, Result>`    | One application operation. Pure given its ports.                 |\n118\t| `Registry<TKey, TValue>`    | One extension axis. Closed-on-key, validated.                    |\n119\t\n120\tA package may add **layer-2 abstracts** when R-BASE-L2 is satisfied. Recommended candidates by\n121\tdemonstrated repetition:\n122\t\n123\t| Layer-2                         | Spine parent | Captures                                    | Required when                    |\n124\t| ------------------------------- | ------------ | ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------- |\n125\t| `ScaffoldCommand`               | `CliCommand` | dry-run/force/yes flags + project root      | ≥ 2 scaffold commands            |\n126\t| `ListCommand`                   | `CliCommand` | output format flag + entry-table render     | ≥ 2 list commands                |\n127\t| `DeployStepCommand`             | `CliCommand` | shared deploy context + lifecycle phase     | ≥ 2 deploy step commands         |\n128\t| `Pipeline<TStep>`               | `UseCase`    | sequenced step iteration + evidence capture | ≥ 1 multi-step user-visible flow |\n129\t| `PipelineStep<TInput, TOutput>` | (none)       | step contract: prepare/execute/inspect      | ≥ 2 steps in any pipeline        |\n130\t| `Manifest<TKey, TValue>`        | `Registry`   | typed key enum + bidirectional file scan    | ≥ 1 file-backed registry         |\n131\t| `OutputRenderer`                | (none)       | render `OutputEvent` to a sink              | ≥ 2 output formats               |\n132\t\n133\tEach layer-2 abstract co-locates with its concretes per R-FOLD-AD-COLOC (folder name = plural\n134\tconcept).\n135\t\n136\t## Archetype 6 v2 rules (R-A6-N1 … R-A6-N13)\n137\t\n138\tThe rules below extend the universal doctrine with CLI-specific constraints. Each is enforced by a\n139\tfitness gate.\n140\t\n141\t| Rule     | Statement                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Gate               |\n142\t| -------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------ |\n143\t| R-A6-N1  | Folder cardinality ≤ 12 children; depth ≤ 4 from `src/`.                                                                                                                                                       | F-16 / F-CLI-25    |\n144\t| R-A6-N2  | Kernel uses horizontal layering; per-surface command code uses vertical (feature) slicing.                                                                                                                     | F-CLI-21           |\n145\t| R-A6-N3  | An abstract with ≥ 2 concretes lives in one plural-named folder with all subclasses.                                                                                                                           | F-17 / F-CLI-30    |\n146\t| R-A6-N4  | A feature folder splits into sub-feature folders when ≥ 2 sibling sub-features exist; not before.                                                                                                              | F-CLI-21           |\n147\t| R-A6-N5  | Composition file body matches the declarative shape: `class XCli extends CliRoot { topLevel() { return [<news>]; } }` plus a single `createXCli(...)` factory. No inline `.command()`/`.option()`/`.action()`. | F-CLI-27           |\n148\t| R-A6-N6  | A multi-step user-visible operation delegates to a `Pipeline<Step>` subclass living in the same feature folder under `steps/`.                                                                                 | F-CLI-21           |\n149\t| R-A6-N7  | Commands emit `OutputEvent`. `console.log`/`console.error`/`console.warn` is allowed only under `kernel/presentation/output/**` and `kernel/adapters/logger/**`.                                               | F-CLI-26           |\n150\t| R-A6-N8  | `Deno.exit` only in `bin/**`. `Deno.cwd`/`Deno.env`/`Deno.build`/`Deno.readDir`/`Deno.readFile`/`Deno.writeFile`/`Deno.Command` only in `kernel/adapters/**` and `bin/**`.                                     | F-CLI-15, F-CLI-16 |\n151\t| R-A6-N9  | Per-layer file naming regex passes: presentation `*-command.ts` or `*-group.ts`; application `<verb>-<noun>.ts`; ports `*-port.ts`; adapters `<tech>-<port>.ts` or `<tech>-<role>.ts`.                         | F-CLI-21           |\n152\t| R-A6-N10 | A package with ≥ 2 extension axes exports `kernel/extension-points.ts` aggregating every `Registry`.                                                                                                           | F-CLI-31           |\n153\t| R-A6-N11 | Barrel files (`mod.ts`/`index.ts`) inside `src/**` subdirectories are forbidden, except declared subpath-export entries and `kernel/extension-points.ts`.                                                      | F-18 / F-CLI-20    |\n154\t| R-A6-N12 | `new <X>(Adapter\\|Resolver\\|Runner\\|Scaffolder\\|Renderer\\|Logger\\|Pipeline\\|Step\\|Registry)\\(` only in `composition.ts`, `deps.ts`, `bin/**`, abstract self-reference, or test fixtures.                       | F-CLI-19           |\n155\t| R-A6-N13 | Per-layer file size: presentation ≤ 150 LOC; application use cases ≤ 250 LOC; adapters ≤ 350 LOC; abstracts ≤ 200 LOC; assets `.template` exempt.                                                              | F-CLI-1, F-CLI-2   |\n156\t\n157\t## Predict-the-folder table\n158\t\n159\tThe structural contract: the auditor opens any folder and predicts its contents from the path alone.\n160\t\n161\t| Folder pattern                                  | Predicted contents                                                                 |\n162\t| ----------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n163\t| `src/kernel/domain/<concept-plural>/`           | one abstract `+` N concretes; no IO; no `Deno.*`                                   |\n164\t| `src/kernel/ports/`                             | `*-port.ts` interface files; no implementations                                    |\n165\t| `src/kernel/application/abstracts/`             | spine + layer-2 abstracts; no concretes                                            |\n166\t| `src/kernel/application/registries/`            | concrete `Registry` subclasses, one per axis                                       |\n167\t| `src/kernel/adapters/<port>/`                   | optional `<port>-base.ts` + concrete adapters                                      |\n168\t| `src/kernel/presentation/abstracts/`            | command/group/root + scaffold/list/step abstracts                                  |\n169\t| `src/kernel/presentation/output/`               | `OutputEvent` types + renderer abstract + concrete renderers                       |\n170\t| `src/kernel/assets/<group>/`                    | `.template` files only; no `.ts`                                                   |\n171\t| `src/kernel/extension-points.ts`                | re-exports every `Registry` class with documentation                               |\n172\t| `src/<surface>/composition.ts`                  | declarative `class extends CliRoot` + `createXCli(...)` factory                    |\n173\t| `src/<surface>/deps.ts`                         | only the imports allowed for the surface (JSR for public, monorepo for maintainer) |\n174\t| `src/<surface>/adapters/`                       | surface-only adapters; one folder per adapter                                      |\n175\t| `src/<surface>/features/<feature>/`             | group file + sub-feature folders, OR command + use case + input                    |\n176\t| `src/<surface>/features/<feature>/<sub>/`       | command, use case, input, optional pipeline + `steps/`                             |\n177\t| `src/<surface>/features/<feature>/<sub>/steps/` | `<verb>-step.ts` files, all extending `PipelineStep`                               |\n178\t| `bin/`                                          | one file per binary; only place `Deno.exit` is called                              |\n179\t\n180\t## Fitness Gates\n181\t\n182\tUniversal gates F-1 … F-15, F-16 (folder cardinality), F-17 (abstract co-location), F-18\n183\t(sub-barrel) apply per the doctrine matrix. Static gates: `deno task check`, `deno task lint`, `deno task fmt:check`.\n230\t2. Universal fitness gates: F-1, F-3, F-5 (CLI-aware), F-6, F-7, F-8, F-9, F-10, F-11, F-12, F-15,\n231\t   F-16, F-17, F-18.\n232\t3. Archetype-6 fitness gates: F-CLI-1 … F-CLI-31. These have no dedicated script; report\n233\t   `PENDING_SCRIPT` with manual/structural evidence, backed by `check-doctrine.ts`\n234\t   (`deno task arch:check`, per-root).\n235\t4. Runtime gates: required when a slice changes a command that starts services, scaffolds projects,\n236\t   or shells out to Aspire, Docker, or process adapters.\n237\t5. Consumer gates: scaffolded-project compile and import smoke tests required when the slice changes\n238\t   generated outputs, exported flows, command names, or help output.\n239\t\n240\t## Skills to Activate\n241\t\n242\t- `netscript-doctrine`\n243\t- `netscript-harness` (when the run is harnessed)\n244\t- `jsr-audit` for any slice touching `mod.ts`, `deno.json` exports, or JSDoc\n245\t- domain skills for generated targets (`aspire`, `deno-fresh`) when relevant\n246\t\n247\t## Read First\n248\t\n249\t1. `docs/architecture/doctrine/06-archetypes.md#archetype-6--cli--tooling-package`.\n250\t2. `docs/architecture/doctrine/05-folder-structure.md` (incl. R-FOLD-CARD, R-FOLD-LAYERING-MODE, R-FOLD-AD-COLOC).\n251\t3. `docs/architecture/doctrine/03-base-and-derived-classes.md` (incl. R-BASE-L2).\n252\t4. `docs/architecture/doctrine/07-composition-and-extension.md` (incl. R-COMP-DECL, R-COMP-EXT-MANIFEST).\n253\t5. The CLI's `mod.ts`, `maintainer.ts`, both `bin/*.ts`, both `<surface>/composition.ts`, the kernel\n254\t   `extension-points.ts`.\n255\t6. The current feature most relevant to the slice (one folder under\n256\t   `src/<surface>/features/<feature>/`).\n257\t7. Relevant debt entries.\n258\t\n259\t## Anti-Patterns to Watch For\n260\t\n261\t- **AP-1** — command pipeline or generated-template monolith.\n262\t- **AP-6** — base flow with concrete orchestration. Spine abstracts are stub-only.\n263\t- **AP-11** — env, filesystem, process, or network access outside `kernel/adapters/**`,\n264\t  `<surface>/adapters/**`, or `bin/**`.\n265\t- **AP-18** — giant string snapshots instead of semantic checks on generated output.\n266\t- **AP-19** — permissions and external tool requirements absent from the README permissions block.\n267\t- **AP-21** — flat command-surface folder. Every `**/presentation/**` or `**/features/**` folder\n268\t  respects R-A6-N1 and R-A6-N4.\n269\t- **AP-22** — useless re-export barrel. Sub-folder `mod.ts` is forbidden (F-CLI-20).\n270\t- **AP-23** — inline command body in composition. Composition is declarative (R-A6-N5, F-CLI-27).\n271\t- **AP-24** — switch-over-tagged-union for variants. Use a typed registry from\n272\t  `kernel/extension-points.ts`.\n273\t- **AP-25** — side effect in non-edge file. Adapters and binaries own all\n274\t  `Deno.*`/`console.*`/`fetch`.\n275\t\n276\t## False-Done States\n277\t\n278\t- Files moved without splitting: a 1500-LOC pipeline relocated to\n279\t  `src/<surface>/features/.../pipeline.ts` is still AP-1.\n280\t- Surface separation declared but not enforced: `F-CLI-3`/`F-CLI-4` reported `PASS` without a script\n281\t  run; manual evidence is empty.\n282\t- Composition body still calls `.command()` / `.option()` / `.action()` (R-A6-N5 violated).\n283\t- Vertical slicing claimed but `presentation/` retains 30 sibling command files; feature folders not\n284\t  extracted (R-A6-N1, R-A6-N4 violated).\n285\t- Layer-2 abstract introduced with one concrete subclass (R-BASE-L2 violated).\n286\t- `console.log` left in command files because \"it's just for help output\" (F-CLI-26 violated).\n287\t- `Deno.exit` called from a command for an early exit (F-CLI-15 violated; throw `CliExitError`\n288\t  instead).\n289\t- Tests assert string snapshots of generated output without semantic checks (AP-18).\n290\t- Generated project compiles were skipped or asserted only by type-check.\n291\t\n292\t## Rescope Triggers\n293\t\n294\t- A slice that requires splitting an existing monolith first.\n295\t- A slice that touches the spine abstract API (cascades through every concrete).\n296\t- A slice that introduces a new layer-2 abstract (must demonstrate R-BASE-L2 satisfaction with two\n297\t  existing concretes).\n298\t- A command vocabulary change (verb names, group names).\n299\t- Generated project validation reveals template/runtime drift.\n300\t- A surface boundary change (kernel ↔ public ↔ maintainer).\n301\t- Folder-cardinality violation discovered mid-slice; the structural fix exceeds the slice envelope.\n302\t\n303\t## Design Checkpoint Expectations\n304\t\n305\tThe design checkpoint names: command surface, public flow APIs, extension axes (with their registry\n306\tclasses), generated outputs, adapters, permission requirements, semantic test strategy,\n307\tgenerated-project validation, and consumer impact.\n308\t\n309\tThe design section in `worklog.md` must include:\n310\t\n311\t- The five spine abstract names and their type parameters.\n312\t- The layer-2 abstracts the package introduces, each with the two existing concretes that justify it\n313\t  (R-BASE-L2).\n314\t- The vertical-feature catalog: every `<surface>/features/<feature>/` folder, its sub-features, and\n315\t  its commands.\n316\t- The extension axes: every registry, its key type, its value type, where it is populated, where it\n317\t  is consumed.\n318\t- Ports for command execution, file system, process, HTTP probing, templates, prompts, output\n319\t  rendering.\n320\t- Constants for command names, exit codes, output formats.\n321\t- Composition declarativity contract: which file owns the top-level command list (R-A6-N5).\n322\t- Slice ordering: kernel domain + ports + abstracts first; registries + adapters next; features one\n323\t  at a time; surface composition last.\n324\t- Contributor path for adding (a) a new variant on an extension axis, (b) a new feature, (c) a new\n325\t  sub-feature inside an existing feature, (d) a new template.\n326\t\n327\t## Concept of Done\n328\t\n329\tBeyond the universal slice checklist in `workflow/run-loop.md`:\n330\t\n331\t- the package's `src/` matches the greenfield shape (or every deviation is documented);\n332\t- every spine and layer-2 abstract is stub-only / R-BASE-L2 compliant;\n333\t- every command file is presentation only and ≤ 150 LOC;\n334\t- every composition file passes R-A6-N5 (declarative shape);\n335\t- every folder under `src/` passes R-A6-N1 (cardinality ≤ 12, depth ≤ 4);\n336\t- every abstract with ≥ 2 concretes passes R-A6-N3 (co-located);\n337\t- the kernel `extension-points.ts` lists every `Registry`;\n338\t- `bin/*.ts` is the only place that calls `Deno.exit`;\n339\t- `kernel/presentation/output/**` + `kernel/adapters/logger/**` are the only files that call\n340\t  `console.*`;\n341\t- `deno task arch:check` runs all F-CLI-1 … F-CLI-31 (or every one that is `PENDING_SCRIPT` has\n342\t  manual evidence);\n343\t- `deno doc` reports 100% coverage on `mod.ts`;\n344\t- `deno publish --dry-run` succeeds (modulo declared `JSR_DEPS_PENDING`);\n345\t- generated projects compile and pass smoke imports;\n346\t- the README documents permissions and the `Archetype 6 v2 deviations` section is empty or every\n347\t  entry is matched in `arch-debt.md`.\n348\t\n349\t## Historical Notes\n350\t\n351\t- v1 archetype permitted \"thin presentation, fat application, infra adapters\" without naming the\n352\t  failure modes. In practice this produced flat command folders (AP-21), inline composition (AP-23),\n353\t  and base classes that orchestrated lifecycle (AP-6).\n354\t- v2 names the rules R-A6-N1 … R-A6-N13 and binds each to a fitness gate. Evaluator findings now\n355\t  reference rule IDs, not prose.\n356\t- Run `refactor-cli-doctrine-rewrite` (Phase 11) is the first application of v2 to `@netscript/cli`\n357\t  and is the reference implementation.\n358\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"815534c7-6c02-4aa5-ab86-a905a0bade6f","uuid":"3f1a5da5-3fbc-40da-a2c1-3e1346913a2a","timestamp":"2026-08-06T13:02:47.561Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/codex/repos/ns1331-qwen-evaluator/.llm/harness/archetypes/ARCHETYPE-6-cli-tooling.md","content":"# Archetype 6 — CLI / Tooling (v2)\n\n> v2 amendment supersedes v1. v1 was a generic \"thin presentation, fat application, infra adapters\"\n> rule and proved too permissive in practice (flat 30-file presentation folders, inline command\n> bodies in composition, base classes that orchestrated lifecycle). v2 names the structural rules\n> that prevent each failure mode and binds them to executable fitness gates.\n\n## Doctrine Reference\n\n- Axioms: A1, A2, A4, A5, A6, A7, A8, A9, A10, A11, A13, A14.\n- Primary sections:\n  - `docs/architecture/doctrine/02-public-surface.md`\n  - `docs/architecture/doctrine/03-base-and-derived-classes.md` (incl. R-BASE-L2)\n  - `docs/architecture/doctrine/05-folder-structure.md` (incl. R-FOLD-CARD, R-FOLD-LAYERING-MODE, R-FOLD-AD-COLOC)\n  - `docs/architecture/doctrine/06-archetypes.md#archetype-6--cli--tooling-package`\n  - `docs/architecture/doctrine/07-composition-and-extension.md` (incl. R-COMP-DECL, R-COMP-EXT-MANIFEST)\n  - `docs/architecture/doctrine/09-anti-patterns-and-fitness-functions.md`\n- Anti-patterns: AP-1, AP-2, AP-3, AP-4, AP-5, AP-6, AP-7, AP-8, AP-9, AP-11, AP-13, AP-14, AP-15,\n  AP-16, AP-18, AP-19, AP-20, AP-21, AP-22, AP-23, AP-24, AP-25.\n- Universal fitness functions: F-1, F-2, F-3, F-4, F-5, F-6, F-7, F-8, F-9, F-10, F-11, F-12, F-15,\n  F-16, F-17, F-18. F-14 excludes allowed CLI presentation output.\n- Archetype-specific fitness functions: F-CLI-1 … F-CLI-31 (see §\"Fitness Gates\").\n\n## When This Archetype Applies\n\nUse this profile for packages that ship a binary, command flows, scaffolding, deployment tooling, or\nother user-run automation. The CLI may also expose a library surface (`mod.ts`), but only when\nconsumers legitimately call the flow programmatically; otherwise the only public artifact is the\nbinary.\n\n## Layering modes (R-FOLD-LAYERING-MODE)\n\nArchetype 6 mixes both layering modes:\n\n- **Kernel** is _horizontal_: pure types, ports, application abstracts\n  - registries, adapters, presentation abstracts, output renderers, assets. Each folder names a\n    technical role.\n- **Surfaces** (`public/`, `maintainer/`) are _vertical_: each feature is a folder that contains the\n  command, the use case, the input shape, optionally a pipeline + steps, optionally per-feature\n  adapters.\n- The kernel-surface boundary is enforced by F-CLI-3 (no surface ↔ surface imports) and F-CLI-4\n  (kernel never imports from surfaces).\n\n## Greenfield Folder Shape\n\n```\npackages/<cli-pkg>/\n├── deno.json\n├── mod.ts                                  # JSR public surface\n├── maintainer.ts                           # gated; never published\n├── testing.ts                              # in-memory adapters, fixtures\n├── bin/\n│   ├── <cli>.ts                            # public binary\n│   └── <cli>-dev.ts                        # maintainer binary\n└── src/\n    ├── kernel/                             # horizontal core\n    │   ├── domain/\n    │   │   ├── errors/                     # CliError + concretes\n    │   │   ├── values/                     # value types, ids, paths\n    │   │   └── <axis-plural>/              # one folder per polymorphic family\n    │   ├── ports/                          # *-port.ts only\n    │   ├── application/\n    │   │   ├── abstracts/                  # spine + layer-2 abstracts\n    │   │   └── registries/                 # one Registry concrete per axis\n    │   ├── adapters/\n    │   │   ├── file-system/\n    │   │   ├── logger/\n    │   │   ├── process/\n    │   │   ├── workspace-resolvers/\n    │   │   ├── scaffolders/\n    │   │   ├── templates/\n    │   │   └── <other-port>/               # one folder per port\n    │   ├── presentation/\n    │   │   ├── abstracts/                  # CliCommand, CliCommandGroup,\n    │   │   │                               # CliRoot, ScaffoldCommand,\n    │   │   │                               # ListCommand, DeployStepCommand\n    │   │   └── output/                     # OutputEvent + renderers\n    │   ├── assets/                         # all .template files; typed registry\n    │   │   ├── manifest.ts\n    │   │   ├── template-registry.ts\n    │   │   └── <group>/                    # contracts, plugins, services, …\n    │   └── extension-points.ts             # R-COMP-EXT-MANIFEST\n    ├── public/                             # vertical features\n    │   ├── composition.ts                  # declarative; R-COMP-DECL\n    │   ├── deps.ts                         # imports of jsr:@netscript/* only\n    │   ├── adapters/                       # public-only adapters (e.g. JSR)\n    │   └── features/\n    │       └── <feature>/\n    │           ├── <feature>-group.ts      # if multi-sub-feature\n    │           ├── <sub-feature>/\n    │           │   ├── <verb>-<noun>-command.ts\n    │           │   ├── <verb>-<noun>.ts            # use case\n    │           │   ├── <verb>-<noun>-input.ts\n    │           │   └── steps/                       # if pipeline\n    │           │       └── <verb>-step.ts\n    │           └── …\n    └── maintainer/                          # same shape; monorepo deps\n        ├── composition.ts\n        ├── deps.ts\n        ├── adapters/                        # maintainer-only adapters\n        └── features/\n            └── <feature>/…\n```\n\nThe shape is normative: deviations require an explicit override in the package's README §\"Archetype\n6 v2 deviations\" with rationale, and the deviation is recorded in `arch-debt.md` until resolved.\n\n## Spine and Layer-2 Abstracts\n\nEvery Archetype 6 package declares the **five spine abstracts** (stub-only, per §3 doctrine):\n\n| Spine class                 | Role                                                             |\n| --------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `CliCommand<Input, Result>` | A single user-facing command. Owns one verb.                     |\n| `CliCommandGroup`           | A non-leaf node grouping commands by domain (`contracts`, `db`). |\n| `CliRoot`                   | The composition entry. Owns the top-level command list.          |\n| `UseCase<Input, Result>`    | One application operation. Pure given its ports.                 |\n| `Registry<TKey, TValue>`    | One extension axis. Closed-on-key, validated.                    |\n\nA package may add **layer-2 abstracts** when R-BASE-L2 is satisfied. Recommended candidates by\ndemonstrated repetition:\n\n| Layer-2                         | Spine parent | Captures                                    | Required when                    |\n| ------------------------------- | ------------ | ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------- |\n| `ScaffoldCommand`               | `CliCommand` | dry-run/force/yes flags + project root      | ≥ 2 scaffold commands            |\n| `ListCommand`                   | `CliCommand` | output format flag + entry-table render     | ≥ 2 list commands                |\n| `DeployStepCommand`             | `CliCommand` | shared deploy context + lifecycle phase     | ≥ 2 deploy step commands         |\n| `Pipeline<TStep>`               | `UseCase`    | sequenced step iteration + evidence capture | ≥ 1 multi-step user-visible flow |\n| `PipelineStep<TInput, TOutput>` | (none)       | step contract: prepare/execute/inspect      | ≥ 2 steps in any pipeline        |\n| `Manifest<TKey, TValue>`        | `Registry`   | typed key enum + bidirectional file scan    | ≥ 1 file-backed registry         |\n| `OutputRenderer`                | (none)       | render `OutputEvent` to a sink              | ≥ 2 output formats               |\n\nEach layer-2 abstract co-locates with its concretes per R-FOLD-AD-COLOC (folder name = plural\nconcept).\n\n## Archetype 6 v2 rules (R-A6-N1 … R-A6-N13)\n\nThe rules below extend the universal doctrine with CLI-specific constraints. Each is enforced by a\nfitness gate.\n\n| Rule     | Statement                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Gate               |\n| -------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------ |\n| R-A6-N1  | Folder cardinality ≤ 12 children; depth ≤ 4 from `src/`.                                                                                                                                                       | F-16 / F-CLI-25    |\n| R-A6-N2  | Kernel uses horizontal layering; per-surface command code uses vertical (feature) slicing.                                                                                                                     | F-CLI-21           |\n| R-A6-N3  | An abstract with ≥ 2 concretes lives in one plural-named folder with all subclasses.                                                                                                                           | F-17 / F-CLI-30    |\n| R-A6-N4  | A feature folder splits into sub-feature folders when ≥ 2 sibling sub-features exist; not before.                                                                                                              | F-CLI-21           |\n| R-A6-N5  | Composition file body matches the declarative shape: `class XCli extends CliRoot { topLevel() { return [<news>]; } }` plus a single `createXCli(...)` factory. No inline `.command()`/`.option()`/`.action()`. | F-CLI-27           |\n| R-A6-N6  | A multi-step user-visible operation delegates to a `Pipeline<Step>` subclass living in the same feature folder under `steps/`.                                                                                 | F-CLI-21           |\n| R-A6-N7  | Commands emit `OutputEvent`. `console.log`/`console.error`/`console.warn` is allowed only under `kernel/presentation/output/**` and `kernel/adapters/logger/**`.                                               | F-CLI-26           |\n| R-A6-N8  | `Deno.exit` only in `bin/**`. `Deno.cwd`/`Deno.env`/`Deno.build`/`Deno.readDir`/`Deno.readFile`/`Deno.writeFile`/`Deno.Command` only in `kernel/adapters/**` and `bin/**`.                                     | F-CLI-15, F-CLI-16 |\n| R-A6-N9  | Per-layer file naming regex passes: presentation `*-command.ts` or `*-group.ts`; application `<verb>-<noun>.ts`; ports `*-port.ts`; adapters `<tech>-<port>.ts` or `<tech>-<role>.ts`.                         | F-CLI-21           |\n| R-A6-N10 | A package with ≥ 2 extension axes exports `kernel/extension-points.ts` aggregating every `Registry`.                                                                                                           | F-CLI-31           |\n| R-A6-N11 | Barrel files (`mod.ts`/`index.ts`) inside `src/**` subdirectories are forbidden, except declared subpath-export entries and `kernel/extension-points.ts`.                                                      | F-18 / F-CLI-20    |\n| R-A6-N12 | `new <X>(Adapter\\|Resolver\\|Runner\\|Scaffolder\\|Renderer\\|Logger\\|Pipeline\\|Step\\|Registry)\\(` only in `composition.ts`, `deps.ts`, `bin/**`, abstract self-reference, or test fixtures.                       | F-CLI-19           |\n| R-A6-N13 | Per-layer file size: presentation ≤ 150 LOC; application use cases ≤ 250 LOC; adapters ≤ 350 LOC; abstracts ≤ 200 LOC; assets `.template` exempt.                                                              | F-CLI-1, F-CLI-2   |\n\n## Predict-the-folder table\n\nThe structural contract: the auditor opens any folder and predicts its contents from the path alone.\n\n| Folder pattern                                  | Predicted contents                                                                 |\n| ----------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `src/kernel/domain/<concept-plural>/`           | one abstract `+` N concretes; no IO; no `Deno.*`                                   |\n| `src/kernel/ports/`                             | `*-port.ts` interface files; no implementations                                    |\n| `src/kernel/application/abstracts/`             | spine + layer-2 abstracts; no concretes                                            |\n| `src/kernel/application/registries/`            | concrete `Registry` subclasses, one per axis                                       |\n| `src/kernel/adapters/<port>/`                   | optional `<port>-base.ts` + concrete adapters                                      |\n| `src/kernel/presentation/abstracts/`            | command/group/root + scaffold/list/step abstracts                                  |\n| `src/kernel/presentation/output/`               | `OutputEvent` types + renderer abstract + concrete renderers                       |\n| `src/kernel/assets/<group>/`                    | `.template` files only; no `.ts`                                                   |\n| `src/kernel/extension-points.ts`                | re-exports every `Registry` class with documentation                               |\n| `src/<surface>/composition.ts`                  | declarative `class extends CliRoot` + `createXCli(...)` factory                    |\n| `src/<surface>/deps.ts`                         | only the imports allowed for the surface (JSR for public, monorepo for maintainer) |\n| `src/<surface>/adapters/`                       | surface-only adapters; one folder per adapter                                      |\n| `src/<surface>/features/<feature>/`             | group file + sub-feature folders, OR command + use case + input                    |\n| `src/<surface>/features/<feature>/<sub>/`       | command, use case, input, optional pipeline + `steps/`                             |\n| `src/<surface>/features/<feature>/<sub>/steps/` | `<verb>-step.ts` files, all extending `PipelineStep`                               |\n| `bin/`                                          | one file per binary; only place `Deno.exit` is called                              |\n\n## Fitness Gates\n\nUniversal gates F-1 … F-15, F-16 (folder cardinality), F-17 (abstract co-location), F-18\n(sub-barrel) apply per the doctrine matrix. F-14 excludes allowed CLI presentation output (renderers\nand logger adapters).\n\nArchetype-specific gates F-CLI-1 … F-CLI-31:\n\n| Gate     | Asserts F-CLI-* fitness functions have **no dedicated script** — the `check-cli-*.ts` family was deleted\nin S9 (see `gates/fitness-gates.md`). In every phase they are recorded as `PENDING_SCRIPT` with\nmanual/structural evidence, backed by the mechanical `check-doctrine.ts`\n(`deno task arch:check`, per-root).\n\n## Required Gates in Order\n\n1. Static gates: `deno task check`, `deno task lint`, `deno task fmt:check`.\n2. Universal fitness gates: F-1, F-3, F-5 (CLI-aware), F-6, F-7, F-8, F-9, F-10, F-11, F-12, F-15,\n   F-16, F-17, F-18.\n3. Archetype-6 fitness gates: F-CLI-1 … F-CLI-31. These have no dedicated script; report\n   `PENDING_SCRIPT` with manual/structural evidence, backed by `check-doctrine.ts`\n   (`deno task arch:check`, per-root).\n4. Runtime gates: required when a slice changes a command that starts services, scaffolds projects,\n   or shells out to Aspire, Docker, or process adapters.\n5. Consumer gates: scaffolded-project compile and import smoke tests required when the slice changes\n   generated outputs, exported flows, command names, or help output.\n\n## Skills to Activate\n\n- `netscript-doctrine`\n- `netscript-harness` (when the run is harnessed)\n- `jsr-audit` for any slice touching `mod.ts`, `deno.json` exports, or JSDoc\n- domain skills for generated targets (`aspire`, `deno-fresh`) when relevant\n\n## Read First\n\n1. `docs/architecture/doctrine/06-archetypes.md#archetype-6--cli--tooling-package`.\n2. `docs/architecture/doctrine/05-folder-structure.md` (incl. R-FOLD-CARD, R-FOLD-LAYERING-MODE, R-FOLD-AD-COLOC).\n3. `docs/architecture/doctrine/03-base-and-derived-classes.md` (incl. R-BASE-L2).\n4. `docs/architecture/doctrine/07-composition-and-extension.md` (incl. R-COMP-DECL, R-COMP-EXT-MANIFEST).\n5. The CLI's `mod.ts`, `maintainer.ts`, both `bin/*.ts`, both `<surface>/composition.ts`, the kernel\n   `extension-points.ts`.\n6. The current feature most relevant to the slice (one folder under\n   `src/<surface>/features/<feature>/`).\n7. Relevant debt entries.\n\n## Anti-Patterns to Watch For\n\n- **AP-1** — command pipeline or generated-template monolith.\n- **AP-6** — base flow with concrete orchestration. Spine abstracts are stub-only.\n- **AP-11** — env, filesystem, process, or network access outside `kernel/adapters/**`,\n  `<surface>/adapters/**`, or `bin/**`.\n- **AP-18** — giant string snapshots instead of semantic checks on generated output.\n- **AP-19** — permissions and external tool requirements absent from the README permissions block.\n- **AP-21** — flat command-surface folder. Every `**/presentation/**` or `**/features/**` folder\n  respects R-A6-N1 and R-A6-N4.\n- **AP-22** — useless re-export barrel. Sub-folder `mod.ts` is forbidden (F-CLI-20).\n- **AP-23** — inline command body in composition. Composition is declarative (R-A6-N5, F-CLI-27).\n- **AP-24** — switch-over-tagged-union for variants. Use a typed registry from\n  `kernel/extension-points.ts`.\n- **AP-25** — side effect in non-edge file. Adapters and binaries own all\n  `Deno.*`/`console.*`/`fetch`.\n\n## False-Done States\n\n- Files moved without splitting: a 1500-LOC pipeline relocated to\n  `src/<surface>/features/.../pipeline.ts` is still AP-1.\n- Surface separation declared but not enforced: `F-CLI-3`/`F-CLI-4` reported `PASS` without a script\n  run; manual evidence is empty.\n- Composition body still calls `.command()` / `.option()` / `.action()` (R-A6-N5 violated).\n- Vertical slicing claimed but `presentation/` retains 30 sibling command files; feature folders not\n  extracted (R-A6-N1, R-A6-N4 violated).\n- Layer-2 abstract introduced with one concrete subclass (R-BASE-L2 violated).\n- `console.log` left in command files because \"it's just for help output\" (F-CLI-26 violated).\n- `Deno.exit` called from a command for an early exit (F-CLI-15 violated; throw `CliExitError`\n  instead).\n- Tests assert string snapshots of generated output without semantic checks (AP-18).\n- Generated project compiles were skipped or asserted only by type-check.\n\n## Rescope Triggers\n\n- A slice that requires splitting an existing monolith first.\n- A slice that touches the spine abstract API (cascades through every concrete).\n- A slice that introduces a new layer-2 abstract (must demonstrate R-BASE-L2 satisfaction with two\n  existing concretes).\n- A command vocabulary change (verb names, group names).\n- Generated project validation reveals template/runtime drift.\n- A surface boundary change (kernel ↔ public ↔ maintainer).\n- Folder-cardinality violation discovered mid-slice; the structural fix exceeds the slice envelope.\n\n## Design Checkpoint Expectations\n\nThe design checkpoint names: command surface, public flow APIs, extension axes (with their registry\nclasses), generated outputs, adapters, permission requirements, semantic test strategy,\ngenerated-project validation, and consumer impact.\n\nThe design section in `worklog.md` must include:\n\n- The five spine abstract names and their type parameters.\n- The layer-2 abstracts the package introduces, each with the two existing concretes that justify it\n  (R-BASE-L2).\n- The vertical-feature catalog: every `<surface>/features/<feature>/` folder, its sub-features, and\n  its commands.\n- The extension axes: every registry, its key type, its value type, where it is populated, where it\n  is consumed.\n- Ports for command execution, file system, process, HTTP probing, templates, prompts, output\n  rendering.\n- Constants for command names, exit codes, output formats.\n- Composition declarativity contract: which file owns the top-level command list (R-A6-N5).\n- Slice ordering: kernel domain + ports + abstracts first; registries + adapters next; features one\n  at a time; surface composition last.\n- Contributor path for adding (a) a new variant on an extension axis, (b) a new feature, (c) a new\n  sub-feature inside an existing feature, (d) a new template.\n\n## Concept of Done\n\nBeyond the universal slice checklist in `workflow/run-loop.md`:\n\n- the package's `src/` matches the greenfield shape (or every deviation is documented);\n- every spine and layer-2 abstract is stub-only / R-BASE-L2 compliant;\n- every command file is presentation only and ≤ 150 LOC;\n- every composition file passes R-A6-N5 (declarative shape);\n- every folder under `src/` passes R-A6-N1 (cardinality ≤ 12, depth ≤ 4);\n- every abstract with ≥ 2 concretes passes R-A6-N3 (co-located);\n- the kernel `extension-points.ts` lists every `Registry`;\n- `bin/*.ts` is the only place that calls `Deno.exit`;\n- `kernel/presentation/output/**` + `kernel/adapters/logger/**` are the only files that call\n  `console.*`;\n- `deno task arch:check` runs all F-CLI-1 … F-CLI-31 (or every one that is `PENDING_SCRIPT` has\n  manual evidence);\n- `deno doc` reports 100% coverage on `mod.ts`;\n- `deno publish --dry-run` succeeds (modulo declared `JSR_DEPS_PENDING`);\n- generated projects compile and pass smoke imports;\n- the README documents permissions and the `Archetype 6 v2 deviations` section is empty or every\n  entry is matched in `arch-debt.md`.\n\n## Historical Notes\n\n- v1 archetype permitted \"thin presentation, fat application, infra adapters\" without naming the\n  failure modes. In practice this produced flat command folders (AP-21), inline composition (AP-23),\n  and base classes that orchestrated lifecycle (AP-6).\n- v2 names the rules R-A6-N1 … R-A6-N13 and binds each to a fitness gate. Evaluator findings now\n  reference rule IDs, not prose.\n- Run `refactor-cli-doctrine-rewrite` (Phase 11) is the first application of v2 to `@netscript/cli`\n  and is the reference implementation.\n","numLines":358,"startLine":1,"totalLines":358}}}

IMPL-EVAL must not FAIL a slice for retaining either class.\n1180\t\n1181\t### Class A — oRPC router-composition `any` (top-level `router.ts`)\n1182\t\n1183\t- **Reason:** Every plugin service confines one-or-more `// deno-lint-ignore no-explicit-any` casts\n1184\t  to its **top-level router-composition file** (`plugins/<plugin>/services/src/router.ts`): the\n1185\t  `v1: any` version group, `os.prefix('/v1/<plugin>').router(<map> as any)`, and\n1186\t  `router: any = os.router({ v1 })`. The handler map in\n1187\t  `plugins/<plugin>/services/src/routers/v1-handlers.ts` is correspondingly typed\n1188\t  `Record<string, unknown>`. Root cause is the shared contract wrapper: `<plugin>ContractV1` is\n1189\t  built as `implement(<def>) as unknown as <Plugin>ContractV1`, whose `$context<T>()` exposes a\n1190\t  hand-rolled `.handler(fn): TOutput` that returns the handler **output** rather than a typed oRPC\n1191\t  _procedure_, so `os.router(map)` cannot type-check the map without `any`. This is the\n1192\t  consumer-side manifestation of the contract-side debt `workers-contract-structural-server-export`\n1193\t  (structural server-contract shim that weakens deep handler-options inference). Confirmed present\n1194\t  in the merged exemplar `plugins/sagas/services/src/router.ts:39-44,71-75`; the same pattern exists\n1195\t  (or is being made to match it) in `workers`, `triggers`, `streams`, and `auth`.\n1196\t\n1197\t### Class B — external-boundary `as unknown as` / `as <T>` casts (SDK adapters, KV/db clients, bootstrap)\n1198\t\n1199\t- **Reason:** Every plugin service carries pre-existing single-step boundary casts where an\n1200\t  **external or untyped surface** is narrowed to the plugin's internal port type. These are NOT in\n1201\t  handler business logic — they sit at the I/O seam between third-party SDKs / Deno KV / Prisma\n1202\t  clients / plugin-bootstrap context and the service's typed interior. They are arbitrary in the\n1203\t  sense that the external surface cannot be made to flow its own precise type to the port without a\n1204\t  dedicated adapter-typing pass. IMPL-EVAL must not FAIL a slice for retaining either class.\n\n### Class A — oRPC router-composition `any` (top-level `router.ts`)\n\n- **Reason:** Every plugin service confines one-or-more `// deno-lint-ignore no-explicit-any` casts\n  to its **top-level router-composition file** (`plugins/<plugin>/services/src/router.ts`): the\n  `v1: any` version group, `os.prefix('/v1/<plugin>').router(<map> as any)`, and\n  `router: any = os.router({ v1 })`. The handler map in\n  `plugins/<plugin>/services/src/routers/v1-handlers.ts` is correspondingly typed\n  `Record<string, unknown>`. Root cause is the shared contract wrapper: `<plugin>ContractV1` is\n  built as `implement(<def>) as unknown as <Plugin>ContractV1`, whose `$context<T>()` exposes a\n  hand-rolled `.handler(fn): TOutput` that returns the handler **output** rather than a typed oRPC\n  _procedure_, so `os.router(map)` cannot type-check the map without `any`. This is the\n  consumer-side manifestation of the contract-side debt `workers-contract-structural-server-export`\n  (structural server-contract shim that weakens deep handler-options inference). Confirmed present\n  in the merged exemplar `plugins/sagas/services/src/router.ts:39-44,71-75`; the same pattern exists\n  (or is being made to match it) in `workers`, `triggers`, `streams`, and `auth`.\n\n### Class B — external-boundary `as unknown as` / `as <T>` casts (SDK adapters, KV/db clients, bootstrap)\n\n- **Reason:** Every plugin service carries pre-existing single-step boundary casts where an\n  **external or untyped surface** is narrowed to the plugin's internal port type. These are NOT in\n  handler business logic — they sit at the I/O seam between third-party SDKs / Deno KV / Prisma\n  clients / plugin-bootstrap context and the service's typed interior. They are arbitrary in the\n  sense that the external surface cannot be made to flow its own precise type to the port without a\n  dedicated adapter-typing pass. Confirmed in the merged exemplar:\n  `plugins/sagas/services/src/main.ts:89,102` (`dbClient as unknown as PrismaSagaStoreClient`,\n  `... as unknown as SagaStreamPrismaClient`), `v1-helpers.ts` (KV deserialization), and the same\n  class exists across `workers`, `triggers`, `streams`, and `auth`. In `auth` specifically:\n  `services/src/backend-registry.ts` (WorkOS SDK session client, `ArrayBuffer` views),\n  `services/src/init.ts` (`WatchableKv`, `PluginServiceContext`), `services/src/main.ts`\n  (`ServiceDatabaseClient`, the `AuthRunningService` builder return, `PluginServiceBootstrap`).\n- **Owner:** Future cross-plugin oRPC-seam fix branch (one dedicated branch reworking the shared\n  `$context` contract wrapper to the native oRPC `implement(<contractDefinition>).$context<T>()`\n  builder so `.router({...})` returns properly-typed procedures and `os.router(map)` type-checks\n  with ZERO `as any` — AND introducing per-adapter typed shims that retire the Class-B boundary\n  casts). NOT owned by any feature, quality-hardening, or auth slice.\n- **Target:** Post-framework-prime-time, before beta. All plugins migrate together so the contract\n  wrapper and every `router.ts` change in lock-step.\n- **Linked plan:** related contract-side debt `workers-contract-structural-server-export` (above).\n- **Created:** 2026-06-21.\n- **Status:** open, DEBT_ACCEPTED. **SANCTIONED for now** — matching the merged sagas exemplar\n  (casts confined to the top-level router-composition file, each with `deno-lint-ignore`, zero casts\n  elsewhere) is the conformant target for all current slices.\n- **Agent directive (read before failing a slice on this):** Do NOT attempt to remove the Class-A\n  composition `any` casts, un-widen the `Record<string, unknown>` handler maps, change the contract\n  package, OR rewrite the Class-B external-boundary `as unknown as` / `as <T>` casts, inside a\n  feature / quality-hardening / auth slice. Mirroring the sagas exemplar IS conformant and IMPL-EVAL\n  must NOT FAIL a slice for retaining EITHER the sanctioned router-composition casts (Class A) or\n  the pre-existing external-boundary casts (Class B) that match sagas. The only out-of-scope cast\n  error is a **NEW** cast a slice INTRODUCES in handler / business-logic / contract code (outside\n  `router.ts` and outside the established I/O-adapter seams) — those remain strictly zero-cast. All non-long-running, non-complex work; targeted mid-complexity\n  fixes. Start here unless the slice clearly needs more.\n- **medium** — only when there is real potential for additional research or decision-taking\n  mid-slice.\n- **high** — genuinely new features or complex fixes.\n- **max** — escalation tier: architectural / deep-thinking / multiple-possible-outcome work, or\n  tasks left unresolved by a lower-effort agent.\n\nThese efforts map onto the implementation lanes above (`light_implementation` · low,\n`normal_implementation` · medium, `complex_implementation` · high) and drive the effort-paired\nreview pairing; `max` is the escalation tier and, per the forward rule, pairs with a Fable 5 · high\nadversarial review.\n\n### Fable 5 restored as default (2026-07-16)\n\nFable 5 returned to the Anthropic subscription. The audit is opposite-family by design (Codex\n  reviewing Claude-generated docs restores family diversity), so the generator is never the auditor\n  and there is **no cross-family fallback**. Two layers:\n *\n *  - LAYER A (exact, authoritative): the forbidden set is DERIVED from ALL\n *    exported string values of `config/models.ts` + `config/versions.ts` +\n *    `config/endpoints.ts` (via `mod.ts`) — not a hand-maintained subset. Any\n *    such value appearing in a production source is a violation. The one\n *    documented exclusion is `agy` (also the legitimate Antigravity CLI\n *    executable name, used as a path/argv token across `wsl/` and the adapters).\n *\n *  - LAYER B (structural): flags model-/version-/endpoint-SHAPED literals in\n *    production sources even if the exact string is not (yet) in `config/`. This\n *    is what fails when someone introduces a NEW hardcoded model id.\n *\n * SCOPE — what \"production sources\" means here (the claim this test actually\n * enforces): every `*.ts` under the suite EXCEPT `config/**` (the single\n * source) and `*_test.ts`. Test files are NOT blanket-excluded: they are scanned\n * against Layer A and may contain a config value ONLY if explicitly listed in\n * `TESTS_ALLOWED_TO_PIN_CONTRACT_LITERALS`, so the \"single source\" claim stays\n * truthful and a new test cannot silently smuggle a literal. The suite\n * `README.md` is also scanned (Layer A) with an explicit illustrative allowlist.\n * Non-`.ts` operational docs elsewhere are out of scope by design.\n */\n\nimport { assert } from '@std/assert';\nimport * as config from './mod.ts';\nimport { MODEL_IDS } from './models.ts';\nimport { OPENCODE_TOOL } from './versions.ts';\n\nconst suiteRoot = new URL('../', import.meta.url);\n\n// --- Derive the exact forbidden set from ALL exported config string values ----\nfunction collectStrings(value: unknown, out: Set<string>): void {\n  if (typeof value === 'string') out.add(value);\n  else if (value && typeof value === 'object') {\n    for (const inner of Object.values(value)) collectStrings(inner, out);\n  }\n}\nconst configValues = new Set<string>();\ncollectStrings(config, configValues);\n\n/**\n * `agy` (=`MODEL_IDS.antigravity`) is also the Antigravity CLI executable name,\n * used as a path/argv token across `wsl/` and the adapters — it is not a\n * \"hardcoded model id\" in those non-config sources.\n */\nconst OVERLOADED_EXCLUSIONS = new Set<string>([\n  MODEL_IDS.antigravity,\n  // These OpenCode values are also finite domain vocabulary used in typed\n  // contracts and policy data, not independently pinned volatile values.\n  OPENCODE_TOOL.binary,\n  OPENCODE_TOOL.defaultVariant,\n]);\nconst EXACT_FORBIDDEN = [...configValues].filter((v) => !OVERLOADED_EXCLUSIONS.has(v));\n\n// --- Layer B: structural shapes of model ids / versions / endpoints -----------\n// Precise forms that cannot collide with the suite's domain vocabulary (provider\n// profile ids use dash forms like `codex-design-glm-5-2`; Keep this tiny and justified.\n */\nconst STRUCTURAL_ALLOWLIST: Readonly<Record<string, readonly string[]>> = {};\n\n/**\n * Test files that legitimately pin config-derived contract literals (asserting\n * observed behavior).
